[11:04:58] login_id:  | SQL: INSERT INTO tbl_monthly_attendance (member_id, from_date, to_date, attendance, isreceiveble)
                SELECT a.member_id, ? AS from_date, ? AS to_date, COUNT(a.member_id) AS attendance, 0 AS isreceiveble
                FROM tbl_attendance AS a
                WHERE a.date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                  AND a.member_id NOT IN (
                      SELECT tmp.member_id FROM (
                          SELECT member_id FROM tbl_monthly_attendance WHERE from_date = ? AND to_date = ?
                      ) AS tmp
                  )
                GROUP BY a.member_id | Values: ["2025-12-01","2025-12-31","2025-12-01","2025-12-31","2025-12-01","2025-12-31"]
[11:04:58] login_id:  | SQL: UPDATE tbl_monthly_attendance AS ma
                SET ma.attendance = (
                    SELECT COUNT(*) 
                    FROM tbl_attendance AS a 
                    WHERE a.member_id = ma.member_id 
                      AND a.date BETWEEN ma.from_date AND ma.to_date
                )
                WHERE ma.from_date = ? AND ma.to_date = ? | Values: ["2025-12-01","2025-12-31"]
